### [1] [HySpecPro: Scalable Hypergraph Partitioning via Spectral Projection Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00055)
*Rongjian Liang,Zhuo Feng,Haoxing Ren*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: HySpecPro是一种单级超图划分器，在谱嵌入空间中进行端到端优化，实现了与最先进多级方法相当的切割质量，同时随超边总度数线性扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代VLSI设计包含数百亿组件，使得可扩展的超图划分至关重要。虽然多级划分仍是主流范式，但其粗化阶段会扭曲结构信息，特别是在具有许多高度超边的超图中，导致细化开销增加和可扩展性受限。现有方法仅以启发式方式融入谱信息，未直接优化划分目标。

Method: HySpecPro构建基于二部拉普拉斯矩阵的嵌入表示，在谱嵌入空间中执行端到端优化。该方法采用高效的基于投影的搜索策略，并实现了完全GPU加速的实现方案。

Result: 实验结果表明，HySpecPro提供的切割质量与最先进的多级方法相当，同时随超边总度数呈现线性扩展特性。

Conclusion: HySpecPro成功展示了单级谱方法作为传统多级划分范式替代方案的潜力，特别适用于大规模、高度超边密集的超图划分问题，为可扩展的超图划分提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Modern VLSI designs comprise tens of billions of components, making scalable hypergraph partitioning critical for parallel and hierarchical optimization. Although multilevel partitioning remains the dominant paradigm, its coarsening stage can distort structural information, especially in hypergraphs with many high-degree hyperedges, leading to increased refinement overhead and limited scalability. Recent approaches incorporate spectral information to guide coarsening, but only in a heuristic manner, without directly optimizing the partitioning objectives. We introduce HySpecPro, a single-level hypergraph partitioner that performs end-to-end optimization in a spectral embedding space. HySpecPro constructs embeddings from a bipartite Laplacian and performs efficient projection-based search, supported by a fully GPU-accelerated implementation. Experiments show that HySpecPro delivers cut quality comparable to state-of-the-art multilevel methods while scaling linearly with the total hyperedge degree.

</details>


### [2] [High-Performance NTT Accelerators for PQC leveraging Unified Redundant Arithmetic and Fine-Tuned Microarchitecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00621)
*George Alexakis,Dimitrios Schoinianakis,Giorgos Dimitrakopoulos*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于优化统一蝶形单元的并行迭代NTT/INTT加速器，通过新颖的冗余数表示法消除条件校正，并将逆变换缩放集成到运算硬件中，在FPGA上实现了更高时钟频率和更低执行时间，有效支持后量子密码学应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后量子密码学（如ML-KEM和ML-DSA）严重依赖大次数多项式运算，数论变换（NTT）是关键计算原语。现有硬件加速器虽然利用并行和流水线技术支持NTT/INTT，但受限于模归约和校正步骤的开销、逆变换缩放操作以及FPGA实现效率不佳等问题。

Method: 1. 提出基于优化统一蝶形单元的并行迭代NTT/INTT加速器；2. 引入新颖的冗余数表示法，消除Montgomery模乘和组合减法-乘法运算中的条件校正；3. 将逆变换缩放集成到现有算术硬件中，避免专用缩放单元；4. 设计分层Montgomery乘法器，高效映射到FPGA DSP资源，降低硬件成本并支持高工作频率。

Result: FPGA实验结果表明，所提出的加速器实现了更高的时钟频率、更低的执行时间以及具有竞争力的资源利用率，有效支持PQC及相关隐私保护应用中的高效NTT加速。

Conclusion: 通过冗余数表示法和硬件优化设计，有效解决了现有NTT/INTT加速器中的效率瓶颈问题，为后量子密码学和隐私保护应用提供了高效、低开销的硬件加速方案。

Abstract: Post-quantum cryptography and privacy-preserving technologies are expected to play a central role in future secure communication systems. Lattice-based PQC schemes such as ML-KEM (CRYSTALS-Kyber) and ML-DSA (CRYSTALS-Dilithium) rely heavily on large-degree polynomial arithmetic, making the Number Theoretic Transform (NTT) a key computational primitive. Although existing hardware accelerators exploit parallelism and pipelining to support both NTT and INTT, their efficiency is often limited by the overhead of modular reduction and correction steps, inverse-transform scaling operations, and suboptimal FPGA implementations. This work addresses these limitations by proposing parallel iterative NTT/INTT accelerators based on optimized unified butterfly units. We introduce a novel redundant number representation that eliminates conditional corrections for both Montgomery modulo multiplication and combined subtract-multiply operations, and integrate inverse-transform scaling into existing arithmetic hardware to avoid dedicated scaling units. Furthermore, we design hierarchical Montgomery multipliers that map efficiently onto FPGA DSP resources, reducing hardware cost while enabling high operating frequencies. FPGA-based experimental results demonstrate higher clock frequencies, reduced execution times, and competitive resource utilization, supporting efficient NTT acceleration for PQC and related privacy-preserving applications.

</details>

### [3] [SmoothAgent: Efficient Long-Horizon LLM-Based Agent Serving with Lookahead Context Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00151)
*Zaifeng Pan,Qianxu Wang,Zhengding Hu,Chang Chen,Yue Guan,Yanbo Zhou,Steven Swanson,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种前瞻式编程模型，通过异步执行上下文变换来消除LLM代理框架中的变换开销，将TTFT降低多达11.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM代理框架在执行多轮工作流时依赖上下文工程策略（如卸载、缩减和隔离）来控制上下文长度，但这些策略会引入显著的上下文变换开销：每次变换都会使现有的KV缓存失效并触发重新预填充，导致首令牌时间（TTFT）增加。

Method: 利用上下文变换的“段可分解”特性（前缀变换独立于未来token），提出了前瞻式编程模型，允许代理框架将上下文变换表达为异步操作。运行时提前执行这些变换并准备好变换后的KV缓存，实现无阻塞的直接上下文替换。同时设计了支持前瞻感知的调度器，在LLM服务系统中支持这些异步请求与时延敏感工作负载共存。

Result: 实验表明，该方法有效消除了变换开销，将TTFT降低了高达11.9倍。该方法已成功集成到现有的代理框架和LLM服务系统中，支持代表性的上下文工程策略。

Conclusion: 通过识别和利用上下文变换的段可分解特性，前瞻式编程模型能够显著减少LLM代理框架中的上下文变换开销，为多轮工作流场景下的模型服务质量维护提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: LLM-based agents execute multi-turn workflows with continuously growing contexts, where LLM calls are interleaved with tool invocations and environment feedback. To maintain model quality, modern agent frameworks rely on context engineering strategies such as offloading, reduction, and isolation to control the context length. However, these strategies introduce significant context transformation overhead: each transformation invalidates existing KV caches and triggers re-prefill, leading to increased time-to-first-token (TTFT).
  In this paper, we identify that context transformations are segment-decomposable, where the transformation of a prefix is independent of future tokens. This property enables transformations to be executed ahead of time. Based on this insight, we propose a lookahead programming model that allows agent frameworks to express context transformations as asynchronous operations without modifying their execution logic. The runtime proactively executes these transformations and prepares transformed KV caches in advance, enabling direct context replacement without blocking. We further design a lookahead-aware scheduler in LLM serving systems to support these asynchronous requests alongside latency-critical workloads with controlled interference. We implement our approach to support representative context engineering strategies and integrate it into existing agent frameworks and LLM serving systems. Experiments show that our approach effectively eliminates transformation overhead and reduces TTFT by up to 11.9x.

</details>


### [4] [Promise-Future Synchronization for Cluster Asynchronous Many-Task Runtimes via MPI One-Sided Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00303)
*Mia Reitz*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了ItoyoriFBC异步多任务运行时，实现了承诺-未来模型，支持动态创建依赖关系，在分层LU分解算法上实现16节点15.6倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ItoyoriFBC运行时原有的仅未来模型存在两个限制：未来对象在创建时绑定生产者、读取任务数量需编译时固定，无法直接表达动态创建依赖关系的算法。

Method: 在ItoyoriFBC运行时中实现承诺-未来模型，将未来对象的创建与生产者绑定解耦，并允许运行时动态确定读取任务数量，从而支持动态算法表达。

Result: 使用分层LU分解算法在最多16个节点上进行实验评估，观察到接近理想的扩展性，实现了15.6倍的加速比。

Conclusion: 承诺-未来模型的实现成功解决了原有模型的局限性，使ItoyoriFBC能够有效支持具有动态依赖关系的算法，并展现出良好的可扩展性能。

Abstract: Asynchronous Many-Task (AMT) runtimes use futures as placeholders for values produced by other tasks. In the ItoyoriFBC AMT runtime, the existing future-only model binds each future to its producer at creation time and requires the number of tasks that read each future to be fixed at compile time. This prevents directly expressing algorithms that create dependencies dynamically. We extend ItoyoriFBC with an implementation of a promise-future model that lifts these limitations. Thereby, our ItoyoriFBC variant supports dynamic algorithms such as Hierarchical LU factorization (HLU). We experimentally evaluated our implementation using HLU on up to 16 nodes and observed near-ideal scaling with a 15.6x speedup.

</details>


### [5] [CloudyGUI: A Novel Python-based Framework for Auto-Scaling and Cloud Workload Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00455)
*Jyoti Bawa,Mohit Kaushik,Kuljit Kaur Chahal,Kamaljit Kaur*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了CloudyGUI，一个基于Python的云计算资源管理仿真框架，通过GUI界面简化了自动伸缩策略的测试与验证过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云计算环境的高度动态性给资源管理带来了重大挑战，准确的负载预测对于有效的自动伸缩至关重要，但缺乏易用的仿真工具供研究人员测试和验证资源管理策略。

Method: 采用三阶段流水线架构：负载生成、预测（使用XGBoost和LSTM算法）、以及基于MAPE循环的自动伸缩系统；通过内部验证、中间验证和外部验证三种方法确保系统可靠性。

Result: 生成的负载与真实世界数据集高度匹配，双样本K-S检验显示CPU和内存的p值分别为0.19和0.14；与命令行工具相比，GUI仅增加1.4x-4.67x的最小开销；专家评审验证了工具的真实性和实用性。

Conclusion: CloudyGUI填补了一个关键空白，为云应用自动伸缩仿真提供了一个易访问且高效的平台，有助于研究人员开发先进的云管理解决方案。

Abstract: Purpose: Cloud computing environments are highly dynamic, creating major challenges for resource management. Accurate workload prediction is therefore essential for effective auto-scaling. To address this, we present CloudyGUI, a Python simulation framework with an easy-to-use GUI that allows researchers to test and validate resource management strategies. Methods: This framework employs a three-stage pipeline: workload generation, prediction (utilizing XGBoost and LSTM), and an auto-scaling system based on the MAPE loop. Validation includes internal, intermediate, and external methods to ensure system reliability. Results: CloudyGUI's generated workloads closely match real-world datasets. A two-sample K-S test confirms this alignment, showing strong p-values of 0.19 for CPU and 0.14 for memory. When compared to a command-line tool, the GUI adds only a minimal overhead of 1.4x-4.67x. Furthermore, expert review validates the tool's realism and practical usefulness. Conclusion: CloudyGUI fills a critical gap by providing an accessible and efficient platform for simulating auto-scaling in cloud applications, helping researchers develop advanced cloud management solutions.

</details>


### [6] [ELDR: Expert-Locality-Aware Decode Routing for PD-Disaggregated MoE Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00466)
*Sangjin Choi,Sukmin Cho,Yifan Xiong,Ziyue Yang,Youngjin Kwon,Peng Cheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了ELDR，一种面向PD disaggregated MoE服务架构的专家局部性感知解码路由器，通过考虑专家激活模式和负载均衡，有效降低了推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PD disaggregated LLM服务中的解码路由器仅考虑负载均衡，但对于MoE模型这是不充分的：具有相同负载的工作节点可能因每个解码步骤加载的专家权重不同而产生不同的延迟，忽视了专家局部性的影响。

Method: 提出ELDR路由器：(1)利用prefill阶段的专家激活构建专家签名预测生成阶段的专家激活；(2)离线使用平衡K-means将签名空间划分到解码工作节点；(3)在线采用局部性带宽路由将请求发送到最佳匹配签名的最小负载工作节点；(4)设计签名缓存与KV缓存在KV块粒度上协同索引以保持前缀缓存下的精确性。

Result: 在vLLM上实现并在最多40个GPU的部署上评估，ELDR在三种MoE模型和两种工作负载下，相比四种负载均衡基线中最强者，中位TPOT降低5.9-13.9%，且模型输出保持不变。

Conclusion: ELDR证明了在PD disaggregated MoE服务中，同时考虑专家局部性和负载均衡能够显著改善推理性能，为MoE模型的路由优化提供了新的思路。

Abstract: In prefill-decode (PD) disaggregated LLM serving, each request is assigned to a decode worker after prefill. Existing decode routers balance only load; for mixture-of-experts (MoE) models this is incomplete: equally loaded workers can differ in latency, since each decode step loads the weights of every distinct expert its batch activates. We present ELDR, an expert-locality-aware decode router for PD-disaggregated MoE serving. From a request's prefill expert activations, ELDR builds an expert signature predicting the experts it will activate during generation. Offline, balanced K-means partitions signature space across decode workers; online, locality-band routing sends each request to the least-loaded worker among those best matching its signature. A signature cache, co-indexed with the KV cache at KV-block granularity, keeps signatures exact under prefix caching. Implemented in vLLM and evaluated on deployments of up to 40 GPUs, ELDR reduces median TPOT by 5.9-13.9% over the strongest of four load-balancing baselines across three MoE models and two workloads, with model outputs unchanged.

</details>


### [7] [Modeling and Chasing the Energy-Efficiency Sweet Spots in Modern GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00819)
*Ayesha Afzal,Markus Manfred Li,Michael Panzlaff*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了异构CPU-GPU系统中硬件配置对能耗的影响，发现能耗效率存在由非线性GPU功率-频率缩放驱动的工作负载和架构依赖的效率区转换，提出了一个可解释的解析模型来识别转换频率，为能效优化提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能耗是异构CPU-GPU系统高性能计算的关键限制因素。研究旨在理解硬件配置如何在真实工作负载下影响求解能耗，为能效优化提供科学依据。

Method: 使用分子动力学基准测试（GROMACS和AMBER）和压力测试基准（FIRESTARTER），在配备A40、A100、H100和H200 GPU及Intel Ice Lake CPU的系统上，通过改变频率缩放和功率上限来研究能效模式。提出了一个可解释的解析模型，将GPU功率分解为线性和非线性组件。

Result: 求解能耗表现出工作负载和架构依赖的效率区转换，由非线性GPU功率-频率缩放驱动。模型能够以低误差拟合经验数据，识别出转换频率作为关键参数。功率上限在效率调优方面通常不如频率降低有效，特别是对于远离热设计功耗运行的工作负载。

Conclusion: 能效HPC执行是一个配置依赖问题，具有可识别的区转换。基准功率、非线性行为和转换频率是控制能效的主导参数，研究提供了模型驱动的操作点选择指导。

Abstract: Energy consumption is a key limitation in high-performance computing on heterogeneous CPU-GPU systems. This work studies how hardware configuration affects energy-to-solution under realistic workloads. We study energy efficiency regimes using molecular dynamics benchmarks (GROMACS and AMBER) and a stress-test benchmark (FIRESTARTER) on systems with A40, A100, H100, and H200 GPUs and Intel Ice Lake CPU, varying frequency scaling and power cap. We show that energy-to-solution exhibits workload- and architecture-dependent transitions between efficient and inefficient regimes, driven by nonlinear GPU power-frequency scaling. We introduce an interpretable analytical model that decomposes GPU power into linear and nonlinear components, identifying a workload- and architecture-dependent transition frequency beyond which efficiency degrades. The model fits empirical data with low error and highlights the role of baseline power, nonlinear power behavior, and transition frequency as the dominant parameters governing energy efficiency. Power capping is generally less effective for efficiency tuning than frequency reduction, especially for workloads that operate far from thermal design power. Overall, energy-efficient HPC execution is a configuration-dependent problem with identifiable regime shifts, and we provide model-driven guidance for selecting operating points.

</details>


### [8] [Next-Generation Agentic Reinforcement Learning Systems Enable Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01120)
*Ran Yan,Wei Fu,Jiale Li,Shusheng Xu,Zhiyu Mei,Jiaxuan Gao,Jiarui Zhang,Xujie Shen,Hao Dai,Chuyi He,Zhen Pu,Jun Mei,Zhiyao Lin,Haitao Wang,Zhiqiang Ding,Jiawei Zhang,Huaijie Wang,Ruida Xu,Youhe Jiang,Yi Wu,Tongkai Yang,Binhang Yuan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文指出现有企业级LLM智能体部署缺乏持续学习能力，提出了构建智能体在线RL系统的三大关键支柱：标准化轨迹数据协议、企业级数据代理和统一演化控制平面，并实现了AReaL2.0系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前企业级LLM智能体部署存在根本性局限：模型权重、系统提示、工具库和上下文框架在部署时被冻结，任何改进都需要人工数据收集、离线微调和重新部署的循环。虽然OpenClaw等自我演化智能体的研究表明持续学习是能力提升的关键，但企业级大规模智能体服务的这一愿景受到现有智能体在线RL系统的制约，而非RL算法本身的限制。

Method: 提出下一代智能体RL系统需要围绕三个支柱协同设计：(i)标准化智能体轨迹数据协议，能够在异构智能体范式中传递步骤粒度的RL学习信号；(ii)企业级综合数据代理，将真实工作负载转换为受治理的学习底板；(iii)统一智能体演化控制平面，基于轨迹统计自动决定何时更新策略权重或演化上下文框架。实现了AReaL2.0系统，将现有RL基础设施重组为面向智能体的在线RL循环。

Result: 识别并形式化了企业级智能体在线RL系统的三大核心缺陷；提出了针对这三个支柱的具体架构方案、案例研究和反论点；通过AReaL2.0实现了其中一个分支，成功将部署工作负载转化为策略权重更新的在线RL循环。

Conclusion: 企业级自演化智能体部署的关键瓶颈在于缺乏合适的智能体在线RL系统和可观测性软件栈，而非RL算法本身。下一代系统必须围绕标准化轨迹协议、企业级数据治理和统一演化控制三大支柱协同设计，才能实现从部署工作负载中持续学习的智能体服务。

Abstract: LLM agents are rapidly being deployed in production, including coding assistants, customer-support chatbots, and scientific research assistants, yet they remain fundamentally static in enterprise deployment. The LLM weights, system prompts, tool repertoires, and in-context harnesses are frozen at deployment time, and any improvement requires a manual loop of human-curated data collection, offline fine-tuning, modification of the agentic paradigm, and re-deployment. Recent work on self-evolving agents, such as OpenClaw for individual users, indicates that the next leap in agent capability will come from agents that continually learn from their own experience. In this paper, we argue that this vision for self-evolving agent deployment is being held back for enterprise-level large-scale agentic service not by reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms but by agentic online RL systems. Specifically, current agentic RL systems and the surrounding observability software stack are inadequate along three essential aspects: (i) there is no standardized agent trajectory data protocol capable of carrying RL learning signals at step granularity across heterogeneous agent paradigms; (ii) there is no enterprise-grade comprehensive data proxy that converts real workloads into governed learning substrates; and (iii) there is no unified agent evolution control plane that automatically decides, based on trajectory statistics, when to update policy weights or evolve the in-context harness. The next generation of agentic RL systems must be co-designed around these three pillars, and we sketch concrete architectures, case studies, and counter-arguments. We instantiate one branch through AReaL2.0, reorganizing existing RL infrastructure into an agent-oriented online RL loop for policy weight updates from deployed workloads.

</details>

### [9] [MeshDNS: A Cooperative DNS Resolution Framework for Resource-Constrained IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00122)
*Asif Mahbub,Md. Abir Hossain,Nabil Bin Hannan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MeshDNS，一种面向资源受限IoT环境的去中心化协作DNS解析框架，在ESP8266上实现0.47ms热缓存解析，并通过Ed25519签名和仲裁投票机制隔离拜占庭故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网网络中的DNS解析面临资源受限、连接不可靠和安全漏洞等独特挑战，传统中心化DNS架构存在单点故障风险。

Method: MeshDNS采用去中心化架构，节点使用基于哈希的摘要维护缓存感知，通过Ed25519签名和相同答案仲裁投票保障冷缓存缺失的安全性，实现拜占庭故障隔离。

Result: 在ESP8266微控制器（<50KB可用RAM，80MHz）上实现0.47ms热缓存解析，优于原生mDNS基线（1.39ms）；冷缓存缺失安全防护带来约1.3-1.7秒的密码学开销；5节点物理测试床和1000节点离散事件仿真验证了框架在网络震荡下维持弹性本地名称缓存的能力。

Conclusion: MeshDNS成功证明了在资源受限IoT环境中通过可预测的密码学开销实现拜占庭故障隔离的可行性，为持久性边缘遥测提供了弹性的本地名称缓存解决方案。

Abstract: Domain Name System (DNS) resolution in Internet of Things (IoT) networks presents unique challenges due to resource constraints, unreliable connectivity, and security vulnerabilities. Traditional centralized DNS architectures introduce single points of failure. This paper presents MeshDNS, a cooperative DNS resolution framework designed for resource-constrained IoT environments operating under shared-key admission. MeshDNS employs a decentralized architecture where nodes maintain cache awareness using hash-based summaries and secure cold-cache misses via Ed25519-signed, identical-answer quorum voting. Our implementation on commodity ESP8266 microcontrollers (sub-50 KB usable RAM, 80 MHz) achieves a 0.47 ms warm-cache resolution, outperforming native mDNS baselines (1.39 ms). To secure initial cold-cache misses, MeshDNS trades a predictable ~1.3-1.7s cryptographic penalty to successfully isolate Byzantine faults among admitted peers. Assuming a threat model where physical hardware extraction remains out of scope, MeshDNS demonstrates Byzantine fault isolation. We validated the framework via a 5-node physical testbed and discrete-event simulations scaling to 1,000 nodes; the results demonstrate that MeshDNS maintains resilient local name caches for persistent edge telemetry under network churn. Code is available at https://github.com/mahbubasif/MeshDNS-Artifact.

</details>


### [10] [An LLM-Based Framework for Intent-Driven Network Topology Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00292)
*Kholoud El-Habbouli,Fen Zhou,Stephane Huet*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结合层次化建模和系统验证的约束驱动流程，评估LLM生成网络拓扑的能力，并发布了包含四个真实场景的公开数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自然语言需求设计可部署且具有弹性的网络拓扑是网络自动化中的挑战性问题，本文旨在研究LLM生成结构有效且符合约束的网络拓扑的能力。

Method: 提出约束驱动的流水线框架，结合层次化建模与系统验证；对多款私有和开源权重的LLM进行对比实验，在四个真实网络场景上评估，使用节点和边F1分数评估结构正确性，采用服务器和内容连通性指标评估弹性。

Result: 构建了系统基准并发布公开数据集，揭示了LLM在拓扑合成中处理结构与弹性约束的表现，分析了接口不匹配、方向不一致等常见失效模式，支持AI驱动网络设计的模型选择。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解LLM在网络拓扑合成中的能力提供了系统基准，有助于指导面向AI驱动网络设计的模型选择。

Abstract: Designing deployable and resilient network topologies from natural language requirements remains a challenging problem in network automation. This work investigates the ability of Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate structurally valid and constraint-compliant network topologies through a constraint-driven pipeline combining hierarchical modeling and systematic validation. The framework is evaluated via a multimodel comparison of proprietary and open-weight LLMs across four realistic network scenarios released as a public dataset. We assess structural correctness using node and edge F1-scores against reference topologies, and evaluate resilience through server and content connectivity metrics. In addition, we analyze common failure modes, including interface mismatches and directional inconsistencies in generated topologies. Overall, this work provides a systematic benchmark for understanding how LLMs handle structural and resilience constraints in topology synthesis, and supports informed model selection for AI-driven network design.

</details>


### [11] [Robust Base Station Placement in Agricultural IoT via Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00549)
*Gourav Prateek Sharma,Durgesh Singh,James Gross*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对私有5G精密农业网络，提出了一种最大化最坏情况覆盖率的基站优化布局方法，显著提升了农作物全生长期的网络覆盖性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于私有5G NR的精密农业网络中，随着农作物的生长和成熟，传播环境会发生剧烈变化。如何在作物全生长周期内为物联网传感器节点提供可靠连接，是基站部署面临的关键挑战。

Method: 将K基站选址问题建模为最大化最小季节性覆盖问题，在所有作物生长阶段中最大化最坏情况下的覆盖比例。由于每个目标评估需要昂贵的全阶段射线追踪仿真，采用高斯过程贝叶斯优化框架，利用射线追踪数据构建鲁棒目标的概率代理模型进行高效优化。

Result: 在一个包含三个作物带、频率为3.5 GHz的1平方公里多作物农场中，所提方案仅用少于五十次射线追踪评估，即以K=3个基站实现72.8%的最坏情况覆盖率，在全部四个生长阶段均比预算匹配的最先进方法提升至少4.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效解决农业场景中时变传播环境下的鲁棒基站部署优化问题，在有限计算资源下实现了显著的覆盖性能提升，为精密农业网络设计提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Precision-agriculture networks based on private 5G NR should ensure reliable connectivity for IoT sensor nodes throughout the crop growing season, yet the propagation environment changes dramatically as vegetation grows and matures. We formulate $K$-base-station~(BS) placement as a \textit{maximin seasonal coverage} problem that maximizes the worst-case coverage fraction across all crop growth stages. Since each objective evaluation requires expensive ray-tracing simulations across all stages, we adopt a Gaussian-process Bayesian optimization~(GPBO) framework that builds a probabilistic surrogate of the robust objective using ray tracing. On a $1\,\text{km}^2$ multi-crop farm with three distinct crop zones at $3.5\,\text{GHz}$, the proposed scheme achieves $72.8\%$ worst-case coverage with $K{=}3$ BSs in fewer than fifty ray-tracing evaluations, outperforming budget-matched state-of-the-art approaches by at least $4.6\,\text{pp}$ across all four seasonal stages.

</details>


### [12] [Vehicle-to-Grid as a 5G Smart Grid Vertical: Non-Technical Barriers and Implications for Communication Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00589)
*Shangqing Wang,Laura del Rio Carazo,Frank H. P. Fitzek*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过系统综述指出V2G/V2X大规模部署的主要障碍是非技术因素，将商业、治理、社会和基础设施四大领域的壁垒转化为具体的5G通信系统需求，强调V2G通信设计必须从源头整合社会技术约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管V2G和V2X技术已成熟并广泛演示，但大规模部署主要受限于非技术因素而非通信或电力电子限制。针对无线通信领域，需要将业务、治理、社会和基础设施障碍与具体通信系统需求联系起来，重新审视5G使能的智能电网垂直领域设计。

Method: 基于PRISMA指南对2009-2025年间974篇V2G/V2X文献进行系统综述，聚焦162篇实施关键研究；采用四域框架（商业/经济、治理/政策、社会、基础设施/生态）分析非技术壁垒，并进行时间和区域分析；将这些领域转化为V2G垂直领域的具体通信需求。

Result: 时间和区域分析显示2021年后从技术主导转向多学科整合；识别出V2G通信的关键需求包括：细粒度计量与结算、协议互操作性（ISO 15118、OCPP）、隐私设计数据治理、时延与可靠性差异化服务、以及灵活性控制的边云划分；证明5G V2G设计不能仅是纯技术优化任务。

Conclusion: 5G V2G通信架构设计必须从一开始就整合社会技术约束，而非单纯追求技术优化；研究建议需发展可持续的、数据驱动的V2G通信架构，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-Grid (V2G) and broader Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) technologies are technically mature and widely demonstrated, yet large-scale deployment is constrained mainly by non-technical rather than communication or power-electronics limits. This paper targets the wireless communications community and frames V2G as a 5G-enabled smart grid vertical, linking business, governance, social, and infrastructure barriers to concrete communication-system requirements. Building on a PRISMA-guided systematic review of 974 V2G/V2X publications (2009-2025), and 162 implementation-critical studies, we adopt a four-domain framework of non-technical barriers: Business/Economic, Governance/Policy, Social, and Infrastructure/Ecosystem. Temporal and regional analyses show a shift from technical dominance to multidisciplinary integration after 2021. We translate these domains into communication requirements for V2G verticals, including fine-grained metering and settlement, protocol interoperability (e.g., ISO~15118, OCPP), privacy-by-design data governance, latency- and reliability-differentiated services, and edge-cloud partitioning for flexibility control. The results demonstrate that 5G design for V2G cannot be a purely technical optimization task and must integrate socio-technical constraints from the outset, suggesting research directions for sustainable, data-driven V2G communication architectures.

</details>


### [13] [Fluid-Spatiotemporal Stochastic Geometry: Information Flow in Non-Stationary Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00616)
*Wen-Yu Dong,Weiwei Jiang,Song Zhao,Qi Bi,Sheng Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了流体时空随机几何（F-STSG）框架，通过将动态网络拓扑视为离散节点星座的流体动力学极限，研究了非平稳空间网络中信息流的基本极限，并建立了基于最优传输理论的网络动力学反问题求解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的空间网络信息流基本极限研究通常基于平稳空间点过程假设，但该假设无法捕捉节点强度场在时空上连续演化的非平稳区域，因此需要开发能够描述动态网络拓扑的理论框架。

Method: 将动态网络拓扑视为离散节点星座的流体动力学极限，将潜在网络动力学的识别建模为逆边界值问题；利用最优传输中的最小动能原理，证明了控制网络负载压缩演化的标量势场的存在性和唯一性；建立了耦合连续拉格朗日输运与离散欧拉干扰几何的场论公式。

Result: 推导出信息通量向量作为宏观平流的充分统计量，以及物质导数作为拓扑散度的运动学预测器；通过能量密度缩放和源-信道解释表征了非平稳网络极限，揭示了协调开销、拓扑变形和控制信令需求与演化网络拓扑的运动学熵之间的关系。

Conclusion: F-STSG框架成功地将场论方法引入随机几何，为非平稳空间网络的分析提供了理论基础，证明了通过网络负载的压缩演化可以唯一确定控制势场，为网络拓扑的动态调控和性能优化提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: The fundamental limits of information flow in spatial networks are usually characterized under stationary spatial point processes, but this assumption cannot capture non-stationary regimes where the node intensity field evolves continuously in space and time. This paper develops Fluid-Spatiotemporal Stochastic Geometry (F-STSG), treating dynamic network topology as a hydrodynamic limit of the discrete node constellation. We formulate the identification of latent network dynamics as an inverse boundary value problem and, using the minimum kinetic energy principle from optimal transport, establish the existence and uniqueness of a scalar potential field governing the compressive evolution of network load. The resulting field-theoretic formulation couples continuous Lagrangian transport with discrete Eulerian interference geometry. Based on this model, we derive the information flux vector as a sufficient statistic for macroscopic advection and the material derivative as a kinematic predictor of topological divergence. We further characterize non-stationary network limits through energy-density scaling and source-channel interpretation, showing how coordination overhead, topology deformation, and control signaling requirements are linked to the kinematic entropy of the evolving network topology.

</details>


### [14] [5G Configured Grant Scheduling for 5G-TSN Integration for the Support of Industry 4.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00704)
*Ana Larrañaga,M. Carmen Lucas-Estañ,Imanol Martinez,Javier Gozalvez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的配置授权调度方案，用于集成到TSN网络中的5G系统，通过利用TSN提供的流量信息来协调调度决策，显著增加了可满足延迟要求的TSN流数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业4.0范式下，工厂正在向数字化数据生态系统演进，新的工业服务（如数字孪生、扩展现实、协作机器人）需要高度可靠和确定性的通信网络。5G网络具备满足严格延迟和可靠性要求的能力，但其在工业环境中的应用需要与时间敏感网络（TSN）进行有效集成。TSN作为工业4.0环境的标准有线技术，提供确定性服务水平和完美有界的延迟。实现5G-TSN集成的关键挑战是两个网络调度器的协调。

Method: 提出了一种新的配置授权调度方案，该方案利用TSN提供的关于TSN流量特征的信息来协调其决策与TSN的调度。TSN拥有5G-TSN集成网络能力的信息，负责为每个TSN流量流决定路径和调度。5G中的调度必须根据TSN提供的调度决策和信息进行，以保证TSN流量的端到端延迟要求。

Result: 研究表明，所提出的调度方案显著增加了可以满意服务的TSN流的数量。该方案能够有效协调TSN和5G网络的调度决策，满足不同TSN流的延迟要求。

Conclusion: 提出的配置授权调度方案成功实现了5G与TSN网络的调度协调，能够有效利用TSN提供的流量信息来优化5G调度决策。该方案不仅满足了TSN流量的端到端延迟要求，还大幅提升了网络可以支持的TSN流数量，为工业4.0环境中的5G-TSN集成提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Factories are evolving towards digitalized data-based ecosystems under the paradigm of the Industry 4.0 where new industrial services allow the implementation of more robust, resilient and customized manufacturing systems. Such services (e.g., digital twins, extended reality or cooperative robots) will require highly reliable and deterministic communication networks capable of supporting stringent latency and reliability requirements. 5G networks and their future evolution have the necessary capabilities to meet these requirements. However, the use of 5G in industrial environments requires its effective and efficient integration with Time Sensitive Networking (TSN), which is becoming the standard wired technology for Industry 4.0 environments. TSN provides unprecedented deterministic service levels with perfectly bounded latencies. The integration of the industrial 5G and TSN networks will be key to support the flexibility and determinism demanded by the Industry 4.0 paradigm. A critical aspect to achieve this integration is the coordination of the schedulers of both networks. TSN has information about the capabilities of the 5G-TSN integrated network, and it is in charge of deciding the path and scheduling for each TSN traffic flow. The scheduling in 5G must be done according to the scheduling decisions and information provided by TSN to guarantee the end-to-end latency requirements of TSN traffic. In this context, this paper proposes a novel Configured Grant scheduling scheme for 5G integrated into a TSN network that aims to meet the latency requirements of the different TSN flows. The proposed scheme exploits the information provided by TSN about the characteristics of the TSN traffic to coordinate its decision with the scheduling of TSN. This study demonstrates that the proposed scheduling scheme considerably increases the number of TSN flows that can be satisfactorily served.

</details>


### [15] [Common Radio Resource Management Policy for Multimedia Traffic in Beyond 3G Heterogeneous Wireless Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00705)
*M. Carmen Lucas-Estañ,Javier Gozalvez,Joaquin Sanchez-Soriano*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于线性目标函数和规划工具的公共无线资源管理(CRRM)策略，用于异构无线网络中同时为用户分配合适的无线接入技术(RAT)和相应的无线资源数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后3G无线系统将由多种具有不同但互补的性能和技术特性的无线接入技术(RAT)组成。为了利用这种多样性，同时保证不同RAT之间的互操作性和高效管理，需要定义公共无线资源管理(CRRM)技术。

Method: 提出了一种CRRM策略，利用线性目标函数和规划工具，同时为每个用户分配合适的RAT组合以及该RAT内的无线资源数量，以保障其服务质量(QoS)需求。

Result: 通过评估验证了所提出的CRRM技术的有效性，能够同时完成RAT选择和资源分配，满足用户的QoS要求。

Conclusion: 基于线性规划工具的CRRM策略能够有效管理异构无线网络中的多种无线接入技术，实现资源的最优分配，保证用户的服务质量需求。

Abstract: Beyond 3G wireless systems will be composed of a variety of Radio Access Technologies (RATs) with different, but also complementary, performance and technical characteristics. To exploit such diversity while guaranteeing the interoperability and efficient management of the different RATs, common radio resource management (CRRM) techniques need to be defined. This work proposes and evaluates a CRRM policy that simultaneously assigns to each user an adequate combination of RAT and number of radio resources within such RAT to guarantee its QoS requirements. The proposed CRRM technique is based on linear objective functions and programming tools.

</details>


### [16] [Latency-Sensitive 5G RAN Slicing for Industry 4.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00707)
*Jan García-Morales,M. Carmen Lucas-Estañ,Javier Gozalvez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型延迟敏感的5G RAN切片解决方案，通过设计切片和在切片间分配无线资源的方案，满足不同应用（确定性非周期、确定性周期和非确定性流量）的速率和延迟需求，并在工业4.0场景中进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络切片是5G的新范式，可以在公共网络基础设施上创建不同的逻辑网络实例以支持多样化服务。RAN切片对支持延迟敏感或时间关键型应用尤为重要，因为RAN在端到端传输延迟中占很大比重，而现有方案难以同时满足不同流量类型应用的速率和延迟需求。

Method: 提出了一种延迟敏感的5G RAN切片解决方案，包括切片设计机制和无线资源在切片间的划分（或分配）机制。这些机制专门针对满足不同应用的速率和延迟需求进行设计，特别考虑了确定性非周期流量、确定性周期流量和非确定性流量三种类型。

Result: 该延迟敏感的5G RAN切片方案在工业4.0场景中进行了评估验证，该场景普遍存在严格和/或确定性的延迟要求。研究证明该方案能够有效满足不同应用的速率和延迟需求。

Conclusion: 所提出的延迟敏感5G RAN切片解决方案能够有效支持具有严格和确定性延迟要求的工业4.0应用，并且可以扩展支持其他具有延迟敏感或时间关键型应用的垂直行业。

Abstract: Network slicing is a novel 5G paradigm that exploits the virtualization and softwarization of networks to create different logical network instances over a common network infrastructure. Each instance is tailored for specific Quality of Service (QoS) profiles so that network slicing can simultaneously support several services with diverse requirements. Network slicing can be applied at the Core Network or at the Radio Access Network (RAN). RAN slicing is particularly relevant to support latency-sensitive or timecritical applications since the RAN accounts for a significant part of the end-to-end transmission latency. In this context, this study proposes a novel latency-sensitive 5G RAN slicing solution. The proposal includes schemes to design slices and partition (or allocate) radio resources among slices. These schemes are designed with the objective to satisfy both the rate and latency demands of diverse applications. In particular, this study considers applications with deterministic aperiodic, deterministic periodic and nondeterministic traffic. The latency-sensitive 5G RAN slicing proposal is evaluated in Industry 4.0 scenarios where stringent and/or deterministic latency requirements are common. However, it can be evolved to support other verticals with latency-sensitive or time-critical applications.

</details>


### [17] [Mobile Base Station Positioning in Smart Ports Based on Kriged Sparse Measurements and Obstacle Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00709)
*Paulo Furtado Correia,André Coelho,Manuel Ricardo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出DOCKING框架，利用稀疏无线测量构建无线电环境地图(REM)，推断传播障碍物抽象表示，无需障碍物几何数据库即可实现智能港口MIAB的高效部署优化，实测验证预测误差低于3dB，容量增益达150%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能港口无线网络中，集装箱堆叠和工业结构导致动态无线电阻塞，严重影响移动集成接入与回传(MIAB)部署效率。现有方法依赖障碍物地图、几何信息或计算密集的传播模型，适应性有限，且在实际工业环境中难以获取精确的环境几何数据。

Method: 提出DOCKING框架：(1)利用普通克里金法(OKG)从15%空间采样重构RSRP和SINR观测值，构建无线电环境地图；(2)从重构REM推断传播相关障碍物抽象，用紧凑长方体阻塞模型近似主导衰减区域；(3)基于推断几何进行回传感知优化，确定MIAB位置、UE关联和回传选择，采用快速遗传算法在5-15秒内收敛。

Result: 在真实智能港口条件下：(1)REM重构在90%分位数预测误差低于3dB；(2)障碍物表征真阳性覆盖率超85%；(3)稀疏部署容量增益达150%；(4)现场实测验证吞吐量趋势与优化预测一致，证明稀疏无线测量可为障碍物感知MIAB部署提供充分的环境感知能力。

Conclusion: 稀疏无线电测量能够为障碍物频发的工业环境提供足够的环境感知能力，无需障碍物几何数据库即可实现实用的障碍物感知MIAB部署。该方法在降低数据获取成本的同时，保持高预测精度和显著容量增益，为智能港口等复杂工业场景的无线网络规划提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Smart-port wireless networks suffer from dynamic radio blockage caused by container stacks and industrial structures, challenging efficient mobile integrated access and backhaul (MIAB) deployment. Existing approaches rely on obstacle maps, geometry information, or computationally intensive propagation models that limit adaptability. This paper presents DOCKING, a radio environment map (REM)-driven framework that converts sparse radio measurements into optimization-ready obstacle representations for MIAB deployment. The framework infers propagation-relevant obstacle abstractions from reconstructed REMs, eliminating the need for obstacle-geometry databases while relying only on known network parameters and sparse measurements. Reference signal received power (RSRP) and signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) observations are reconstructed using Ordinary Kriging (OKG), and dominant attenuation regions are approximated by compact cuboidal blockage models. The inferred geometry feeds a backhaul-aware optimization that determines MIAB placement, user equipment (UE) association, and backhaul selection. Under realistic smart-port conditions, REM reconstruction achieves prediction errors below 3 dB at the 90th percentile using only 15% spatial sampling, while obstacle characterization exceeds 85% true-positive coverage. Capacity gains reach 150% in sparse deployments, and a fast Genetic Algorithm converges within 5-15 s per network snapshot. A field campaign using real measurements validates the workflow, showing throughput trends consistent with optimization predictions. Results demonstrate that sparse radio measurements provide sufficient environmental awareness for practical obstacle-aware MIAB deployment in obstruction-prone industrial environments.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于动态频谱接入能量检测的SNR自适应最优阈值设计框架，通过最小化总错误概率的闭式解析解，在低SNR环境下显著降低了错误概率，提升了频谱感知的可靠性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的恒虚警率（CFAR）方案仅基于预定义虚警约束确定感知阈值，忽略了信噪比（SNR）的影响，导致在动态频谱接入系统中感知性能受限，尤其是在低SNR环境下错误概率较高。

Method: 提出一种SNR自适应最优阈值设计框架，将阈值优化问题表述为二次表达式，其系数显式表征SNR和样本数的影响；推导出直接最小化总错误概率的闭式解析解，实现异构SNR条件下的自适应阈值选择，无需穷举数值搜索。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与固定阈值和检测约束方案相比，所提方法降低了错误概率，尤其在低SNR环境下性能提升显著；系统分析了SNR和样本数对检测性能的影响，揭示了虚警与漏检之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该框架提升了动态频谱接入系统的感知可靠性与实用适应性，并为安全协作频谱感知（包括区块链辅助聚合机制）奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper proposes an SNR-adaptive optimal threshold design framework for energy detection in Dynamic Spectrum Access (DSA). Unlike conventional constant false-alarm rate (CFAR)-based schemes that determine the sensing threshold solely from a predefined false-alarm constraint, the proposed method directly minimizes the total probability of error by deriving a closed-form analytical solution. The threshold optimization problem is formulated as a quadratic expression whose coefficients explicitly characterize the effects of signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) and number of samples. This analytical structure enables adaptive threshold selection under heterogeneous SNR conditions without exhaustive numerical search. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed approach reduces the error probability compared with fixed-threshold and detection-constrained schemes, particularly in low-SNR regimes. Furthermore, the impact of SNR and number of samples on detection performance is systematically analyzed, providing deeper insight into the trade-off between false alarm and missed detection. The proposed framework improves sensing reliability and practical adaptability in dynamic spectrum access systems. It also establishes a foundation for secure cooperative spectrum sensing, including blockchain-assisted aggregation mechanisms.

</details>


### [19] [Data-driven mitigation of catastrophic forgetting in dynamic physical layer attack detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01041)
*Aleksandra Knapińska,Marija Furdek*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数据驱动的机制，通过平衡模型更新数据集（利用部分历史攻击数据）来应对动态攻击检测系统中的灾难性遗忘问题，在光网络安全数据集上显著降低了模型适应时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光网络作为全球通信的关键基础设施，需要应对不断演变的攻击技术。现有的动态更新入侵检测模型虽然能适应新威胁，但在遭遇长时间未出现的旧攻击时会发生灾难性遗忘；而静态训练的模型虽能可靠检测已知攻击，却无法适应未知攻击模式。因此，入侵检测系统面临着在威胁适应性和长期知识保留之间的基本权衡。

Method: 提出了一种基于数据驱动的方法来应对灾难性遗忘：通过利用部分历史攻击数据来平衡模型更新数据集，并采用基于阈值的机制，在由于攻击模式变化导致准确率下降时触发数据平衡过程。该方法旨在在保持对已知攻击检测能力的同时，增强模型对新威胁的适应性。

Result: 在实验性光网络安全数据集上的应用表明：（1）与不采用数据平衡的动态模型相比，平均模型适应时间减少了37%；（2）与基于神经网络深度增加的文献基线方法相比，适应变化条件并恢复性能所需的数据批次减少了6%。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据平衡方法有效解决了动态攻击检测系统中的灾难性遗忘问题，成功在适应性和知识保留之间取得了平衡，显著提升了模型在应对攻击模式变化时的性能表现，为光网络安全提供了更高效的入侵检测解决方案。

Abstract: Optical networks are critical infrastructure that underpins global communications, and detecting security breaches that jeopardize them is essential to maintaining worldwide connectivity. As malicious actors continuously evolve their attack techniques, dynamically updated intrusion detection models have become a key component of modern defense mechanisms. By incorporating newly acquired telemetry data, these models can adapt to emerging threats while maintaining high detection performance. However, when previously encountered attacks reappear after a prolonged period of absence, adaptive models may fail to recognize them due to the phenomenon of catastrophic forgetting. In contrast, statically trained models can reliably detect attacks represented in the original training data but lack the ability to adapt to previously unseen attack patterns. Consequently, intrusion detection systems face a fundamental tradeoff between adaptability to evolving threats and long-term retention of previously acquired knowledge. In this work, we propose a data-driven mechanism to cope with catastrophic forgetting in dynamic attack detection systems. Our approach balances the model update datasets by using parts of past attack data. We utilize a threshold-based mechanism to trigger data balancing after accuracy drops due to an active attack change. Applied to an experimental optical network security dataset, the proposed approach reduces the average model adaptation time by 37% compared to its dynamic counterpart that does not employ data balancing. Compared to a baseline from the literature that relies on neural network depth increasing, our approach requires 6% fewer data batches to adapt to changing conditions and regain performance.

</details>

### [20] [Constructive Alignment: Governing Preference Dynamics in Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00001)
*Max Kanwal,Caryn Tran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“建构性对齐”范式，将AI对齐重构为对动态演变的人类偏好轨迹的控制问题，而非满足静态偏好。通过结合行为经济学、心理学和建构主义社会理论，将偏好建模为在交互中演化的分层状态变量，强调对齐的核心是治理AI系统如何影响人类偏好的长期形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐方法通常将人类偏好视为固定目标进行推断和优化，但这与大量实证证据相矛盾：偏好是分层的、动态的，并且通过交互构建，尤其是与自适应技术的交互。随着AI系统变得更加持久、个性化和嵌入社会，它们日益参与塑造人们长期关注、重视和认可的内容，因此需要新的对齐范式。

Method: 提出建构性对齐范式，借鉴行为经济学、心理学和建构主义社会理论，将人类偏好建模为在AI交互下演化的分层状态变量。采用控制理论框架进行形式化，其中系统行为和交互设计共同影响世界状态和人类评价状态。

Result: 形式化了对齐的控制理论框架，明确系统行动与交互设计如何联合影响世界状态和人类评价状态的演化；界定对齐应确保的价值轨迹特征：一致性、反思性认可、认知基础、防操纵界限及不确定条件下的赋能。

Conclusion: 对齐的核心不是控制AI行为本身，而是调节AI系统如何影响人类偏好演化，确保价值轨迹保持一致、反思性认可、认知基础、防操纵界限并在不确定性下赋能。对齐问题因此转变为治理长期价值形成而非单纯满足静态偏好的问题。

Abstract: Most approaches to AI alignment treat human preferences as fixed targets to be inferred and optimized. This assumption conflicts with extensive empirical evidence showing that preferences are layered, dynamic, and constructed through interaction--particularly with adaptive technologies. As AI systems become more persistent, personalized, and socially embedded, they increasingly participate in shaping what people attend to, value, and endorse over time. We introduce Constructive Alignment, a paradigm that reframes alignment as a control problem over evolving human preference trajectories rather than static preference satisfaction. Drawing on behavioral economics, psychology, and constructivist social theory, we model preferences as layered state variables that evolve under interaction with AI systems. We formalize this view using a control-theoretic framework in which system actions and interaction design jointly influence both world states and human evaluative states. We argue that alignment is not primarily about controlling AI behavior, but about regulating how AI systems influence the evolution of human preferences--ensuring that value trajectories remain coherent, reflectively endorsed, epistemically grounded, bounded against manipulation, and empowering under uncertainty. Alignment thus becomes a problem of governing long-term value formation rather than simply satisfying static preferences.

</details>


### [21] [Bounded Morality: Defining the Space of Moral Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00002)
*Max Kanwal,Caryn Tran,Patrick Mineault*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“有界道德”框架，将道德问题形式化为道德广度与道德深度两个维度，证明有限资源下的权衡关系，并将伦理理论解释为适应不同需求体制的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统道德认知模型将道义论、后果主义、德性伦理等伦理理论视为静态规则或价值函数，忽视了有限智能体在道德问题上的计算需求与资源约束。作者旨在从计算角度分析有限智能体所面临的道德问题的需求。

Method: 扩展Herbert Simon的有界理性概念，构建“有界道德”形式框架。将道德情境沿两个正交维度形式化：道德广度（被视为具有道德相关性的实体范围）和道德深度（评估其交互所需的推理整合程度）。在有限资源约束下，推导两维度之间的权衡关系，定义道德计算的可行空间，并将伦理理论映射为该空间内适应不同需求体制的局部高效策略。

Result: 框架揭示了有限资源下道德广度与深度之间的不可避免权衡；提出了受约束下的道德遗憾与道德进步的形式概念；表明人工系统的道德对齐依赖于道德推理能力的规模与分配，而非对人类判断的直接模仿。

Conclusion: 伦理理论并非对道德真理的竞争性解释，而是适应不同道德计算需求体制的局部高效策略；道德对齐应关注道德推理能力的配置与规模。

Abstract: Moral cognition has traditionally been modeled as adherence to fixed ethical theories--deontology, consequentialism, virtue ethics--implemented as static rules or value functions. We propose Bounded Morality, a formal framework for analyzing the computational demands of moral problems faced by finite agents. Extending Herbert Simon's notion of bounded rationality, we formalize moral situations along two orthogonal dimensions: moral breadth, the scope of entities treated as morally relevant, and moral depth, the inferential integration required to evaluate their interactions. Limited resources impose an unavoidable tradeoff between these dimensions, defining a feasible space of moral computation. Within this space, ethical theories correspond to locally efficient strategies adapted to different demand regimes rather than competing accounts of moral truth. The framework yields a formal notion of moral regret and moral progress under constraint, and implies that moral alignment in artificial systems depends on the scaling and allocation of moral reasoning capacity rather than on direct imitation of human judgments.

</details>


### [22] [The MMM Data Model -- A Normative Specification for Knowledge Interoperability in a Decentralisable Knowledge Commons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00032)
*Mathilde Noual*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MMM（一种知识文档化数据模型），旨在突破传统文档中心化信息系统的局限性，通过少量规范性约束与自由文本标签的结合，实现跨学科、跨应用的知识组织与互操作性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息系统以文档为中心，虽适合大规模传播，但限制了知识的结构化、更新、共享与重用；形式化方法虽能部分解决该问题，但因过度强调形式化结构而忽视可用性与适用范围，难以被广泛采用；现有AI系统虽改变文档生产方式，却未提供统一的、可移植的替代方案。

Method: 提出MMM数据模型，融合少量规范性约束与自由文本标签的表达自由，设计上强调跨学科、跨应用、跨部署的互操作性，无需语义收敛；并提供参考实现与试点部署数据以验证可行性与可用性。

Result: 通过参考实现与试点部署数据的演示，表明MMM具备可实现性与初步可用性，能够在不要求语义统一的前提下支持跨领域的知识文档化。

Conclusion: MMM为跨学科协作研究中的知识文档化提供了一种可行的数据模型，弥补了传统文档中心化系统与形式化方法的不足，为知识表达与交换提供了统一、可移植的替代方案。

Abstract: Many information systems are built around documents: self-contained units optimised for print production and linear reading. While effective for large-scale dissemination, the document-centric organisation constrains how knowledge can be structured, updated, shared, and reused. Formal approaches address some of these limitations but struggle to achieve widespread contribution and adoption due to their prioritisation of formal structure over other system properties such as human usability and scope. AI systems are reshaping document production, but without providing a unified portable alternative to traditional documents for humans' expression and exchange of knowledge. This paper presents MMM, a data model for knowledge documentation that emerged from the practical needs of interdisciplinary collaborative research, and positioned here within a comparative analysis of the design space of information systems. MMM combines a small set of normative constraints with the expressive freedom of free-text labels. It is designed for interoperability across disciplines, applications and deployments without requiring semantic convergence. A reference implementation and pilot deployment data demonstrate implementability and early usability.

</details>

*Yashar Talebirad,Eden Redman,Ali Parsaee,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在刘易斯信号博弈中，记忆架构比信道容量更能决定LLM智能体的协调成功。拥有持久私有笔记本的智能体在高容量下表现最佳(0.867±0.023)，而无状态智能体则在高词汇量时崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究两个智能体如何从零开始发明共享语言。在刘易斯信号博弈中，发送者和接收者仅依靠交互历史来协调编码，研究者希望理解记忆架构和信道容量如何影响这一过程。

Method: 研究了五种记忆架构在不同信道配置下的表现，使用LLM智能体进行刘易斯信号博弈实验。比较了有持久私有笔记本的智能体与无状态智能体的表现，并用信息瓶颈理论进行预测分析。

Result: 记忆架构比信道容量更重要：有持久私有笔记本的智能体在容量=25时达到最佳协调(0.867±0.023)，能利用超额信道容量并避免高容量崩溃；无状态智能体在中等容量达到峰值后随词汇增长而退化。信息瓶颈预测的最优容量(=8)反而成为脆弱点，超额容量通常更好。

Conclusion: 仅凭信道容量无法预测协调效果；记忆架构决定了智能体能否将交互历史转化为稳定惯例。理解信号如何演变为语言需要同时考虑信道容量和记忆架构两个维度。

Abstract: How do two agents invent a shared language from scratch? In a Lewis signaling game, a sender and receiver must coordinate on a code using only their interaction history. We study five memory architectures across varying channel configurations with LLM agents and find that memory architecture matters more than channel capacity. Agents with a persistent private notebook benefit from surplus channel capacity and avoid the high-capacity collapse seen in stateless agents, achieving the most reliable coordination ($0.867 \pm 0.023$ at capacity = 25). Stateless agents peak at moderate capacity and then degrade as the vocabulary grows beyond what a rolling context window can track The notebook externalizes learned conventions, freeing agents from having to re-derive codes each round. An information bottleneck-inspired argument predicts an optimal capacity equal to the number of objects. Instead, the bottleneck (capacity = 8) proves to be a fragility point, and surplus capacity is generally better. We show that channel capacity alone cannot predict coordination; memory architecture determines whether agents turn interaction history into stable conventions, and both dimensions are needed to understand how signals become language.

</details>

*Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种约束式、可验证的智能体框架，将大语言模型输出从自由形式代码转变为类型化JSON收集器配置，用于可靠地生成网页爬虫。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出约束式可验证智能体框架，将LLM输出从自由形式代码转为类型化JSON收集器配置。结合六类收集器分类体系、模板与工具函数约束、静态Airflow DAG执行、规则化质量检查以及结构化反馈修正机制。

Conclusion: 该框架为重复性开放网络数据采集提供了可复用、低成本、可验证的执行路径，适用于定期调度采集场景。

Abstract: LLMs and agents can generate web scrapers from natural-language requirements, but direct generation remains unreliable because of dependency errors, broken selectors, schema mismatches, and heterogeneous page structures. We propose a constrained, verifiable agent framework that shifts LLM output from free-form code to typed JSON collector configurations, combining a six-type collector taxonomy, template and utility-function constraints, static Airflow DAG execution, rule-based quality checking, and structured feedback correction. Experiments on 138 tasks show that the taxonomy supports description-based requirement typing, while confirming that stable instantiation requires completing source, field, and execution constraints beyond the initial description. On 80 independently source-verified tasks, the framework runs with zero execution-stage LLM tokens and the lowest average wall-clock time, trading moderate one-shot quality for a reusable, deterministic, and verifiable execution path suited to repeated scheduled collection. These results position the framework as a reusable, low-cost, and verifiable execution path for repeated open-web data collection.

</details>


### [25] [Agri-SAGE: Simulation-Grounded Multi-Agent LLM for Context-Aware Agricultural Advisory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00454)
*Vedant Balasubramaniam,Geetha Charan,Manojkumar Patil,Rohit P Suresh,V Priyanka,Kodur Sai Vinay Sathvik,Y. Narahari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agri-SAGE框架通过整合检索增强的多智能体LLM推理与APSIM生物物理模拟，生成并验证农艺建议，在10年回顾性分析中显著优于静态指导方案，其中Reflexion方法在大幅降低计算成本的同时实现了可比的农艺成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有农艺咨询系统存在根本性矛盾：静态农艺指南虽提供一致的循证建议，却对季节内变异性和动态不确定性缺乏响应；而近期基于LLM的咨询系统则面临生成建议在农艺上可信但在生理上不可信的风险。

Method: 提出Agri-SAGE闭环框架，将检索增强的多智能体LLM推理与基于APSIM的生物物理模拟相结合，用于生成和验证农艺建议；评估了三种推理方法：Plan-and-Solve、Tree of Thoughts和Reflexion。

Result: 在10年回顾性分析中，三种推理方法均显著优于静态PoP（Package-of-Practice）基线；Tree of Thoughts实现了显著的峰值产量；Reflexion通过利用跨季节情景记忆，在大幅降低计算成本的同时实现了相当的农艺成果。

Conclusion: Agri-SAGE框架成功解决了静态指导和LLM动态生成的双重局限性，通过生物物理模拟验证确保建议的生理合理性，其中Reflexion方法在效率与效果之间取得了最佳平衡。

Abstract: Agricultural advisory systems face a fundamental tension: static agronomic guidelines offer consistent, evidence-based recommendations, yet remain blind to in-season variability and dynamic uncertainties. Recent advisory systems powered by LLMs are liable for a different risk of generating recommendations that are agronomically credible but physiologically unconvincing. Agri-SAGE is a closed-loop framework designed to resolve the above two limitations by integrating retrieval-grounded multi-agent LLM reasoning with APSIM-based biophysical simulation, to generate and validate agronomic advisories. To assess this framework, we evaluate three reasoning approaches, namely Plan-and-Solve, Tree of Thoughts, and Reflexion, over a 10-year retrospective analysis. All three significantly outperform static PoP (Package-of-Practice) baselines, with Tree of Thoughts achieving impressive peak yields. At the same time, Reflexion achieves comparable agronomic outcomes at substantially lower computational cost by leveraging cross-seasonal episodic memory.

</details>


### [26] [Solution space path planning for supporting en-route air traffic control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00064)
*Yiyuan Zou,Wenying Lyu,Clark Borst*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种面向航路空管的可解释、计算高效的冲突解脱路径规划算法，通过集成三种意图冲突检测方法并设计SSPPV和SSPPE两种搜索变体，在MUAC Delta扇区场景下实现平均3.69毫秒的计算速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路径规划算法在战术控制中的实际应用有限，揭示了算法设计优先级与空管员需求之间的错位。存在对本质上可解释、计算高效且明确为人使用的决策支持解决方案的需求。

Method: 基于解决方案空间显示框架，集成三种意图驱动的冲突检测方法（基于距离、时间间隔和区域）；提出基于顶点和基于边的搜索节点，开发SSPPV和SSPPE两种路径规划变体；算法设计遵循可解释性、灵活性及管制员决策逻辑，满足间隔标准、机动性限制、航路点最小化和路径实用性等运营约束。

Result: 在基于MUAC Delta扇区的运营相关场景中，SSPPV结合基于区域冲突检测方法取得最佳性能，使用5海里网格平均计算时间为3.69毫秒，验证了算法的计算效率和解决方案质量。

Conclusion: 本研究成功开发了一种与空管员需求兼容的冲突解脱路径规划算法，通过解决方案空间框架实现了可解释性与计算效率的平衡，为战术空管决策支持系统的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As technology advances, many path-planning algorithms have been proposed for Air Traffic Management, yet their operational adoption in tactical control remains limited, revealing a misalignment between algorithmic design priorities and air traffic controllers' needs. This underscores the need for decision-support solutions that are inherently interpretable, computationally efficient, and explicitly designed for human use. Focusing on this design challenge, this study develops a conflict-free path-planning algorithm for en-route Air Traffic Control (ATC) designed to be compatible with two guiding considerations: (1) the interpretability and flexibility offered by solution-space displays, which motivate constructing an algorithm that exposes all feasible safe actions and accommodates shifting optimization goals; and (2) the decision logic controllers naturally apply when enforcing operational constraints, such as separation standards, maneuverability limits, waypoint minimization, and routing practicality. Centered on these principles, the algorithm integrates three intent-based conflict detection methods -- distance-based, time-interval-based, and zone-based -- within a solution-space framework to identify conflict-free paths in computationally efficient ways. Additionally, vertex-based and edge-based search nodes are proposed for solution space path planning (SSPP), resulting in two variants -- SSPPV and SSPPE, respectively, which are evaluated in terms of computational speed and solution quality. Empirical results show that SSPPV paired with zone-based conflict detection achieves the best performance, computing paths in 3.69 ms on average in operational-relevant scenarios based on the Delta sector of the Maastricht Upper Area Control Centre (MUAC) using a 5 nmi grid.

</details>


### [27] [AutoMem: Automated Learning of Memory as a Cognitive Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01224)
*Shengguang Wu,Hao Zhu,Yuhui Zhang,Xiaohan Wang,Serena Yeung-Levy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoMem框架，将记忆管理视为可学习的技能，通过自动化优化记忆结构和模型记忆能力，在三个长周期游戏中实现2-4倍性能提升，使32B模型媲美前沿系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受认知科学中元记忆（metamemory）概念启发，作者认为记忆管理（知道编码什么、何时检索、如何组织知识）是一种可学习的技能。现有LLM智能体在长周期任务中面临记忆管理的双重挑战：记忆结构（提示词、文件模式、动作词汇）和模型执行记忆操作的能力都难以通过人工优化，因为长周期任务可能运行数千步，单个记忆错误可能在很久之后才显现，人工审查完整轨迹不切实际。

Method: 提出AutoMem框架，包含两个自动化循环：(1) 结构优化循环：使用强LLM审查完整的智能体轨迹，迭代修正记忆结构（包括提示词、文件模式、动作词汇），塑造智能体与记忆文件的交互方式；(2) 能力优化循环：从多个训练轮次中识别智能体的优秀记忆决策，将其作为训练信号直接提升模型的记忆能力。关键创新是将文件系统操作提升为一类记忆动作，让模型自主决定如何管理记忆。

Result: 在三个程序生成的长周期游戏（Crafter、MiniHack、NetHack）上进行评估，仅优化记忆管理（不改变模型的任务行为），使基础智能体性能提升约2-4倍。32B开源权重模型的性能达到与Claude Opus 4.5和Gemini 3.1 Pro Thinking等前沿系统相当的水平。

Conclusion: 记忆管理是一种可独立学习的技能，是长周期任务中的高杠杆优化目标。通过将记忆管理视为可训练的技能并自动化优化记忆结构和模型能力，可以在不修改任务行为的情况下实现显著的性能提升。这为LLM智能体的记忆系统设计提供了新视角。

Abstract: Memory expertise is a learned skill: knowing what to encode, when to retrieve, and how to organize knowledge--a capacity known in cognitive science as metamemory. We bring this perspective to LLMs by treating memory management as a trainable skill. We promote file-system operations to first-class memory actions alongside task actions, letting the model itself decide how to manage its memory. This memory skill improves along two axes: the structure that supports it (prompts, file schemas, action vocabulary), and the proficiency of the model exercising it. Both axes resist manual optimization: episodes in long-horizon tasks run for thousands of steps, and a single memory mistake can hide long before it surfaces, making human review of full trajectories impractical. We introduce AutoMem, a framework that automates both axes. In the first loop, a strong LLM reviews complete agent trajectories and iteratively revises the memory structure that shapes how the agent interacts with its memory files. In the second loop, the agent's own good memory decisions are identified from many episodes and used as training signal to sharpen the model's memory proficiency directly. Across three procedurally generated long-horizon games (Crafter, MiniHack, and NetHack), optimizing memory alone--without modifying the model's task-action behavior--improved the base agent's performance ~2x-4x, bringing a 32B open-weight model competitive with frontier systems such as Claude Opus 4.5 and Gemini 3.1 Pro Thinking. Our results show that memory management is an independently learnable skill, and a high-leverage objective yielding large gains on long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [28] [RareDxR1: Autonomous Medical Reasoning for Rare Disease Diagnosis Beyond Human Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00147)
*Deyang Jiang,Haoran Wu,Ziyi Wang,Yiming Rong,Yunlong Zhao,Ye Jin,Bo Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了RareDxR1，一个面向开放域罕见病诊断的端到端推理中心型大语言模型，通过知识内化和自主进化学习框架，结合反思增强推理采样和双级课程强化学习，在多个基准测试中实现了最先进的诊断准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见病鉴别诊断是一项关键但艰巨的临床任务，现有AI方法依赖流水线式的表型提取或检索增强生成，存在因预定义本体导致的严重信息丢失、检索瓶颈以及缺乏诊断逻辑等关键局限性。

Method: 提出RareDxR1模型，采用渐进式端到端训练框架，协同知识内化与自主进化学习；设计反思增强推理采样策略(RERS)，通过从失败中学习合成专家级诊断轨迹；提出双级课程强化学习方法，逐步掌握罕见病诊断能力。

Result: RareDxR1在不同基准测试中均达到了最先进的准确率，在开放域罕见病诊断任务上取得了显著突破。

Conclusion: 该研究成功构建了一个不依赖结构化表型和封闭集决策的端到端罕见病诊断系统，通过将碎片化罕见病知识深度内化到模型参数中，有效解决了现有方法的局限性，代码和数据集将公开发布。

Abstract: Rare disease differential diagnosis is a critical yet arduous clinical task, requiring physicians to identify precise phenotypes from complex, unstructured patient symptoms and execute intricate reasoning within a vast search space. However, existing AI approaches typically rely on pipeline-based phenotype extraction or retrieval-augmented generation, which suffer from critical information loss due to predefined ontologies, retrieval bottlenecks, and a lack of diagnostic logic. To address these challenges, we introduce RareDxR1, an end-to-end reasoning-centric large language model designed for open-domain rare disease diagnosis directly from unstructured clinical notes. We design a progressive end-to-end training framework by synergizing knowledge internalization with autonomous evolutionary learning, thereby bypassing reliance on structured phenotypes and closed-set decision-making. To overcome the limitations of RAG and phenotype restriction, we enabled the deep internalization of fragmented rare-disease knowledge directly into the model's parameters. Moreover, to bridge the gap between model generation and expert reasoning, we propose Reflection-Enhanced Reasoning Sampling (RERS), a strategy that synthesizes expert-level diagnostic trajectories by learning from failures without human annotation. Additionally, we propose a dual-level curriculum reinforcement learning approach for gradually mastering rare disease diagnosis. Experimental results demonstrate that RareDxR1 achieves state-of-the-art accuracy across different benchmarks, marking a significant breakthrough in open-domain rare disease diagnosis. Our code and dataset will be publicly available.

</details>


### [29] [A Contextual-Bandit Oversight Game with Two-Sided Informational Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00155)
*Yunjin Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究人类与AI代理之间的运行时监督问题，发现在双方信息不对称的情况下，非可信监督沟通会导致可避免的伤害区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自主机器人或软件代理检查人类监督者无法直接评估的情况时，会产生自然的信息不对称：人类私下知道自己的奖励函数，而AI私下知道其提议行动的质量。这种双向信息不对称下的监督机制需要深入研究。

Method: 基于合作逆强化学习（CIRL）和监督博弈，构建了一个具有双向信息不对称的上下文赌博机团队博弈模型，采用play/ask/trust/oversee界面。赌博机结构消除了物理状态转移，从而获得精确的一次性特征刻画。提出了团队最优策略和行为上自然的近视规则两种一次性特征刻画。

Result: 发现了两种策略之间存在一个"可避免伤害"差距区域：AI私下知道提议行动是有害的且关闭会有帮助，但近视的人类根据先验信任拒绝监督。证明这个差距是非可信监督沟通的代价。给出了动态过程中通过被动学习和主动信号传导解决该差距的部分分析，采用一期滞后的监督响应机制。

Conclusion: 双向信息不对称下的监督存在非可信沟通问题，导致可避免的伤害区域。该问题可通过多轮重复互动中的被动学习和主动信号传导部分缓解，为实际AI监督系统设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study runtime human oversight of an AI agent when private information runs in both directions: the human privately knows her reward function, while the AI privately knows the quality of the action it proposes. This is the kind of asymmetry that arises naturally when an autonomous robot or software agent has inspected a situation its human supervisor cannot directly assess. Building on Cooperative Inverse Reinforcement Learning (CIRL) and the Oversight Game, we introduce a contextual-bandit team game with two-sided asymmetric information and a play/ask/trust/oversee interface. The bandit structure removes physical state transitions and thereby yields exact one-shot characterizations that would remain conjectural in the full POMDP setting, though the common belief remains a dynamically controlled state across rounds. We give two one-shot characterizations, a team optimum and a behaviorally natural myopic rule, whose gap is a slab of avoidable harm: a region in which the AI privately knows the proposed action is harmful and shutdown would help, yet a myopic human, trusting her prior, declines to oversee. We show this gap is the price of non-credible oversight communication, and give a partial analysis of how it resolves dynamically over repeated rounds through passive learning and active signaling with a one-period-lagged oversight response.

</details>


### [30] [Constructing Epistemic AI Literacy: Detecting Epistemic Aims and Processes in Student-AI Co-Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00211)
*Mengqian Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出认知AI素养（EAIL）框架，通过分析大规模人机协同编程对话数据，发现78.8%的学生交互缺乏认知AI素养，仅11.1%展现出高认知参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能（GenAI）在编程教育中的应用日益广泛，学习者在构建查询、评估验证AI输出及调节问题解决策略时需要进行认知思考。然而，现有AI素养研究缺乏对这一认知过程的深入理解，因此需要重新审视AI素养作为一种过程导向的认知现象。

Method: 基于AIR（认知目标、理想和可靠的认知过程）框架，构建认知AI素养（EAIL）概念框架，并利用大规模人机协同编程对话数据集，识别认知目标（如掌握导向目标）和认知过程（如外包、解释寻求、验证寻求、提示监控和认知论证）的可观察维度，实现这些构念的可扩展操作化。

Result: 研究发现学生普遍缺乏认知AI素养：78.8%的学生-GenAI交互依赖非掌握导向目标和较不可靠的认知策略（如外包和验证寻求）；仅11.1%的交互展现出高认知参与度，表现为掌握导向目标与高级认知策略（如认知论证）的结合。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前学生在GenAI支持的编程活动中认知参与度不足的现状，强调了培养认知AI素养的重要性，为教育实践提供了可操作的观察维度和评估方法。

Abstract: Epistemic thinking plays a central role in students' learning processes when applying generative artificial intelligence (GenAI), particularly in programming contexts where learners must construct queries, evaluate and validate AI-generated outputs, and regulate problem-solving strategies. This study introduces the conceptual framework of Epistemic AI Literacy (EAIL), reframing AI literacy as a process-oriented epistemic phenomenon that emerges through dynamic human-AI interactions across different domains. Drawing on the AIR (epistemic aims, ideals and reliable epistemic processes) framework, this study examines how epistemic aims and epistemic processes are enacted in GenAI-supported co-programming activities and explores scalable approaches for operationalizing these constructs in interaction data. Using a large dialogue dataset of human-AI co-programming, this study identifies observable dimensions of epistemic aims (i.e., mastery-oriented aims) and epistemic processes (i.e., outsourcing, explanation seeking, verification seeking, prompt monitoring, and epistemic justification). The results reveal a prevalent lack of EAIL, with 78.8% of student-GenAI interactions relying on non-mastery-oriented aims and less reliable epistemic strategies like outsourcing and verification-seeking. Conversely, only 11.1% of interactions showed high epistemic engagement, where mastery-oriented aims were coupled with advanced epistemic strategies like epistemic justification in a more reliable epistemic process.

</details>


### [31] [Seed2.0 Model Card: Towards Intelligence Frontier for Real-World Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00248)
*Bytedance Seed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Seed2.0模型系列，通过构建基于真实需求的评估系统，重点解决了长尾知识和复杂指令遵循两大挑战，并在推理、视觉理解和搜索能力上达到世界领先水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在解决现有模型在处理复杂现实任务时的可靠性问题，特别是长尾知识缺失和复杂指令遵循能力不足这两个持续存在的挑战，以满足用户在真实复杂场景下的实际需求。

Method: 首先识别用户的真实需求，构建面向未来的可靠评估系统，通过筛选和抽象化处理基准测试来模拟现实复杂场景；然后在此评估系统的指导下，针对长尾知识和复杂指令遵循两大挑战进行针对性优化。

Result: Seed2.0在复杂长周期任务上显著提升了模型可靠性，在推理智能、视觉理解和搜索能力方面达到世界领先水平，并通过大量真实用例证明其初步具备处理复杂现实任务的能力。

Conclusion: Seed2.0迈出了有意义的一步，开始展现出处理复杂现实任务的能力，能够为数以亿计的用户创造更大价值，朝向解决复杂现实任务的目标迈进。

Abstract: We present Seed2.0, a model series that takes a meaningful step toward solving complex, real-world tasks. Our approach begins with identifying users' genuine needs and constructing a reliable, forward-looking evaluation system by selecting and abstracting benchmarks grounded in these needs and in realistic, complex scenarios. Guided by this evaluation system, Seed2.0 targets two persistent challenges, long-tail knowledge and complex instruction following, substantially improving the model's reliability on intricate, long-horizon tasks. Beyond these, Seed2.0 delivers world-leading reasoning intelligence, visual understanding, and search capabilities that address the most common needs of a broad user base. Through extensive real-world use cases documented in this model card, we demonstrate that Seed2.0 begins to exhibit the ability to handle initial complex real-world tasks, delivering greater value to hundreds of millions of users.

</details>


### [32] [Mnemosyne: Agentic Transaction Processing for Validating and Repairing AI-generated Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00269)
*Edward Y. Chang,Longling Geng,Emily J. Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Transaction Processing (ATP)事务模型，将LLM等生成的动作视为非受信提案，仅在满足声明约束集C时才被运行时准入提交，并实现Mnemosyne系统验证其安全性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs、求解器和智能体团队越来越多地生成工作流动作、修复和计划，但生成的动作在语法上可能有效却存在过期、不可行、冲突或破坏触发修复的证据等问题，需要一种机制来确保生成动作的安全性。

Method: 提出ATP事务模型，将生成动作作为非受信提案处理，需通过声明约束集C的确定性准入检查；实现Mnemosyne运行时，包含仅追加转移日志、有效状态投影、依赖安全补偿和活动提交记录，以及局部修复协议(LCRP)。

Result: 证明了相对C的四个安全属性(权限分离、串行等价生成准入、证据保留修复、义务包含)和有界响应修复保证；实验显示在9个证伪测试中拒绝目标违规，仍能准入有效工作，投影验证开销低于6%，有界局部修复编辑比全局重计算少一个数量级的操作。

Conclusion: ATP通过将生成动作作为非受信提案并引入确定性准入机制，实现了相对于约束集C的提交状态正确性独立于提议层的能力、诚实度或学习，为AI生成动作的安全执行提供了有效的事务处理框架。

Abstract: LLMs, solvers, and agent teams increasingly generate workflow actions, repairs, and plans, but a generated action may be syntactically valid yet stale, infeasible, conflicting, or destructive of the evidence that triggered a repair. We introduce Agentic Transaction Processing (ATP), a transaction model that treats generated actions as untrusted proposals until they pass deterministic admission under a declared, executable constraint set C. The principle is two-sided: a proposal is not truth, and no proposal foresees every disruption: anything may propose, but only the runtime admits and commits, and when an unforeseen disruption strikes it repairs reactively within bounds rather than trusting a fresh proposal. Relative to C, committed-state correctness becomes independent of the competence, honesty, or learning of the proposing layer. We realize ATP in Mnemosyne, a runtime with an append-only transition log, effective-state projection, dependency-safe compensation, and active commitment records, and prove four safety properties relative to C (authority separation, serial-equivalent generative admission, evidence-preserving repair, and obligation containment) together with a bounded-reactive-repair guarantee for its localized repair protocol (LCRP). A reproducible artifact rejects the targeted violations across nine falsification tests while still admitting valid work, at under 6% projection-and-validation overhead, and bounded local repair edits an order of magnitude fewer operations than global recompute. Mnemosyne is open source: https://github.com/eyuchang/Mnemosyne/tree/arxiv-atp-rq1-rq9b-r8-v2.

</details>


### [33] [Managed Autonomy at Runtime: Gear-Based Safety and Governance for Single- and Multi-Agent Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00334)
*Srini Ramaswamy,Wang Miaosheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出\system{}离散时间控制系统，通过五档执行机制解决自主智能体在无人监督下的安全违规、行为不稳定和连续性丢失问题，在机器人装配实验中异常检测率达99.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体（包括LLM驱动的软件代理和机器人物理代理）在缺乏持续人工监督时面临一类共同故障模式：未验证行动导致的安全违规、无约束循环引起的行为不稳定，以及未处理错误状态造成的连续性丢失。这些故障模式严重威胁自主系统的可靠运行。

Method: 开发\system{}离散时间控制系统，结合五档执行机制（\Gobs{}、\Gsug{}、\Gplan{}、\Gexec{}、\Gint{}）与效用门控调度及事件驱动回退机制。单智能体情况下证明单调稳定性、执行安全性、最终稳定性和回退完整性；多智能体信息物理系统采用\smart{}管理自治生命周期，通过共识门控、群体Lyapunov分析、单智能体档位权限和会合控制实现分布式安全稳定性保障。

Result: 在三智能体UR5机器人装配单元上评估，使用NIST机器人手臂位置精度退化测量数据集校准的故障幅度进行10,000次蒙特卡洛实验。异常检测率达99.6%（单智能体基线仅2.1%），检测延迟降低3.5倍，并提供形式化物理工作空间安全证书。在声明假设下实现零碰撞保证。

Conclusion: 执行档位充当\smart{}运行时治理状态之下的微观权限，有效分离行动控制与自治治理。该方法为自主智能体在无人监督环境下的安全可靠运行提供了理论保证和实用框架。

Abstract: Autonomous agents, whether LLM-driven software agents or robotic physical agents, face a common class of failure modes when operating without continuous human oversight: safety violations from unverified actions, behavioral instability from unconstrained loops, and continuity loss from unhandled error states. We develop \system{}, a discrete-time control system that combines five execution gears (\Gobs{}, \Gsug{}, \Gplan{}, \Gexec{}, \Gint{}) with utility-gated dispatch and event-driven fallback. For the single-agent case, we prove monotonic stability, execution safety, eventual stabilization, fallback completeness, and equivalence to a gear-constrained Markov decision process. For multi-agent cyber-physical systems (CPS), we apply the established \smart{} managed-autonomy lifecycle and map runtime evidence into its four governance states (\Stable{}/\Meta{}/\Assisted{}/\Regulated{}). Consensus gating, swarm-level Lyapunov analysis, per-agent gear authority, and rendezvous control provide distributed safety and stability guarantees, including zero collision under the stated assumptions. We evaluate the resulting runtime on a three-agent UR5 robotic assembly cell using fault magnitudes calibrated from the NIST \emph{Degradation Measurement of Robot Arm Position Accuracy} dataset across 10,000 Monte Carlo episodes. It achieves a 99.6\% anomaly detection rate versus 2.1\% for the single-agent baseline, reduces detection latency by $3.5\times$, and supplies a formal physical-workspace safety certificate. The execution gears act as micro-level permissions beneath the \smart{} runtime governance states, separating action control from autonomy governance.

</details>

Method: 提出SPIRE框架，将页面级幻灯片个性化建模为逆向规划问题。通过有意破坏干净幻灯片的视觉结构创建可验证的去噪任务，两个智能体通过强化学习协作优化可执行设计。论文证明了结构去噪是页面级幻灯片个性化的一致替代，且多智能体公式严格减少了强化学习中的策略梯度方差。

Result: 理论证明表明结构去噪是页面级幻灯片个性化的一致替代任务，多智能体公式能够严格降低策略梯度方差。大量实验证明了SPIRE框架的优越性。

Conclusion: SPIRE成功解决了页面级幻灯片个性化问题，通过不假设任何执行工具知识的方式学习设计意图，为细粒度幻灯片设计提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Slide design requires personalizing both deck themes and page layouts. Yet, current AI agent-based methods struggle with fine-grained, page-level design. Solely relying on prespecified templates or user verbose instructions, they fail to capture latent design intents, leaving Page-level Slide Personalization (PSP) unresolved. To close this gap, this work formulates PSP as an inverse planning problem. We propose to learn a design intent without assuming any knowledge of the specific executing tools (e.g., PowerPoint, Beamer) being used. However, relinquishing control over these tools makes the problem intractable to optimize end-to-end. To overcome this, we propose SPIRE, a principled framework to solve PSP approximately. By intentionally corrupting the visual structures of clean slides, SPIRE creates a verifiable task to denoise the corruption, whereby two agents learn to collaboratively refine executable designs via reinforcement learning (RL). We present a proof that structural denoising is a consistent surrogate for PSP, and that the multi-agent formulation strictly reduces policy gradient variance in RL. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of SPIRE.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文引入PHREEQC-MCQ-200基准测试，用于评估工具增强型大语言模型在确定性水-地球化学模拟任务中的表现。研究发现工具访问显著提高整体准确率，但存在性能回退问题，且输出访问策略对不同能力模型的影响存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型代理越来越多地连接到科学软件，但工具访问是否真正使科学计算更加可靠（而非仅仅更加复杂）尚不明确。需要建立评估框架来系统性诊断科学计算中工具增强代理的真实能力与局限。

Result: 工具访问显著提升整体准确率，证实了接地执行对科学计算任务的必要性。但收益非单调：工具增强代理会在无工具时正确作答的题目上出错，揭示了平均准确率掩盖的性能回退。输出访问协议影响显著：目录界面能为强模型降低token成本并保持或提升准确率，但对无法可靠导航结构化输出的中层模型会降低性能。

Conclusion: PHREEQC-MCQ-200将科学工具使用框架化为端到端诊断问题，而非简单的工具调用能力。科学代理评估应报告准确率、项目级保留率、输出访问敏感性、轨迹失败以及计算链断裂位置等多维指标。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly connected to scientific software, yet it remains unclear when tool access makes scientific computation more reliable rather than merely more complex. We introduce PHREEQC-MCQ-200, a benchmark for evaluating tool-augmented agents on deterministic aqueous-geochemistry simulations. The benchmark contains 200 multiple-choice questions derived from 21 validated PHREEQC scenarios, requiring agents to construct simulator inputs, execute PHREEQC, inspect structured outputs, and commit to final answers.
  Across multiple frontier and mid-tier model families, simulator access substantially improves aggregate accuracy, confirming that grounded execution is necessary for many scientific-computation tasks. However, the gains are not monotonic: tool-augmented agents also lose items they answered correctly without tools, revealing regressions that average accuracy alone hides. We further show that output-access protocol matters. A table-of-contents interface can reduce token cost while preserving or improving accuracy for stronger models, but it degrades performance for mid-tier models that cannot reliably navigate structured simulator outputs.
  PHREEQC-MCQ-200 therefore frames scientific tool use as an end-to-end diagnostic problem rather than a simple tool-calling capability. We argue that evaluations of scientific agents should report not only accuracy, but also item-level retention, output-access sensitivity, trajectory failures, and where the computation chain breaks.

</details>

TL;DR: 提出MuSix框架，通过尺度感知的世界模型混合与演进机制，解决了具身智能体在动态环境中多尺度推理与知识适应的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体在现实世界中需要进行多尺度推理和知识适应。现有Mixture of Experts (MoE)方法存在两大局限：路由机制缺乏显式的尺度概念，无法实现针对性的尺度更新；统一的更新策略无法适应不同尺度知识过时的不同速率。

Method: 提出MuSix框架，包含两个核心机制：1）两阶段路由机制，首先利用元路由器将体验距离（一种受建构水平理论启发的情境新颖性度量）映射到连续尺度空间的权重，然后由尺度内基础路由器选择对应尺度的世界模型；2）尺度依赖的遗忘机制，允许低尺度知识快速更新而高尺度抽象知识持久保持，并通过门控跨尺度传递维持层级一致性。

Result: 在EmbodiedBench和HAZARD基准测试上的实验表明，MuSix在多尺度推理和动态适应任务上均优于现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: MuSix通过显式引入尺度感知机制和差异化更新策略，有效提升了具身智能体在动态变化环境中的推理与适应能力，为构建更鲁棒的具身AI系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Embodied agents operating in the real world require multi-scale reasoning and knowledge adaptation as conditions change. We identify two challenges in applying Mixture of Experts (MoE) to this setting: routing lacks an explicit notion of scale, preventing targeted updates at specific scales, and a uniform update policy cannot accommodate the different rates at which knowledge at each scale becomes outdated. We present MuSix, a framework that addresses both challenges through scale-aware world model mixture and evolution. A two-stage routing mechanism grounds scale selection in experiential distance, a measure of situational novelty inspired by Construal Level Theory: a meta-router first maps this quantity to a weight over continuous scale space, then per-scale base routers select world models within the identified scale. For adaptation, scale-dependent forgetting rates allow low-scale knowledge to refresh rapidly while high-scale abstractions persist, and gated inter-scale transfer maintains coherence across the hierarchy. Experiments on EmbodiedBench and HAZARD show that MuSix improves over state-of-the-art baselines on multi-scale reasoning and dynamic adaptation.

</details>

*Zhiyue Xu,Fandi Meng,Kaijie Xu,Clark Verbrugge,Simon Lucas,Jian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文定义了AI原生游戏：运行时生成式AI必须构成核心游戏循环，移除AI会导致游戏形式根本改变。基于此定义筛选了53个AI原生游戏，提出了G/N双轴分类法，发现当前主要集中在语言优先设计，并指出核心设计问题是如何将语义开放性组织为稳定的游戏玩法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI已能实时生成对话、任务、角色等内容，但单纯的生成能力并不构成AI原生游戏，也不能保证可玩性。现有研究缺乏对AI原生游戏的明确定义，无法清晰区分AI原生游戏与AI增强游戏、聊天机器人、程序化内容生成等其他形式，因此需要一个明确的标准来界定和分析这一新兴游戏类别。

Method: 提出反事实标准定义AI原生游戏：如果移除或简单替换AI组件，核心游戏形式会崩溃或发生根本改变。基于此标准筛选候选作品，分析53个公开的AI原生游戏和原型。构建双轴G/N分类法：G轴捕捉面向玩家的游戏类型，N轴捕捉使生成式AI不可或缺的主导AI机制。对语料库进行系统性分析和分类。

Result: 当前AI原生游戏集中在语言优先设计，尤其是叙事冒险、认知交互和生成叙事类型；而语义裁决、多智能体模拟、生成构建、关系/伴侣游戏等类别代表性不足。识别出核心设计问题：如何将语义开放性组织成稳定的游戏玩法。AI原生设计依赖于机械不变量（目标、规则、状态、反馈、节奏、玩家能动性），使开放式的AI输出可解释且有意义。

Conclusion: AI原生游戏设计需要在语义开放性和游戏稳定性之间找到平衡，关键在于建立机械不变量作为设计基础。未来研究方向包括可控生成、AI作为机制的设计、多模态和多智能体系统、推理经济学、评估方法、安全性和监管等七个方面，为AI原生游戏的发展提供路线图。

Abstract: Generative AI now enables games to produce dialogue, quests, characters, images, and worlds at runtime. Yet generation alone does not make a game AI-native, nor does it guarantee playability. This paper defines AI-native games by whether runtime generative AI is constitutive of the core loop: if the AI component were removed or trivially replaced, the central form of play would collapse or become fundamentally different. This counterfactual criterion separates AI-native games from AI-augmented games, boundary artifacts, chatbots, tavern-style role-play, procedural content generation, and AI-assisted production. Using this definition, we screen candidate artifacts and analyze 53 publicly available AI-native games and prototypes. We introduce a dual-axis G/N taxonomy: the G-axis captures player-facing game type, while the N-axis captures the dominant AI mechanic that makes generative AI indispensable to play. The corpus is concentrated around language-forward designs, especially narrative adventure, epistemic interaction, and generative narrative, while categories such as semantic adjudication, multi-agent simulation, generative construction, and relationship/companion play remain less represented. We argue that the central design problem is organizing semantic openness into stable gameplay. AI-native design depends on mechanical invariants: goals, rules, state, feedback, pacing, and player agency that make open-ended AI outputs interpretable and consequential. We conclude with a roadmap for controllable generation, AI-as-mechanic design, multimodal and multi-agent systems, inference economics, evaluation, safety, and regulation.

</details>

*Shei Pern Chua,Fangzhao Wu*
